The pure nodes associated to August seem to demonstrate a seasonality effect, with August 2021 also including a COVID-19 effect (as the membership in 2020 increases) – note that vaccines in the USA became available to the general public beginning in May 2021, so the community associated to August 2021 may include some of the “normal” seasonal effects. We include further discussion in the supplementary materials.

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

Figure 5
:
Pure node memberships for the airport mode, with pure nodes ATL (top left), LAX (top right), and LGA (bottom left). Red demonstrates high membership and purple demonstrates low membership within that particular community. The pure nodes are drawn with large triangles.

Plotted in
Fig.

5
are the membership intensities in each of the communities associated to the three different pure nodes, with red corresponding to high membership and purple corresponding to low memberships. The three pure nodes were found to be ATL (Atlanta), LAX (Los Angeles), and LGA (New York). From the figure it is evident that the LGA community is associated with flights on the eastern half of the country, and LAX is associated with flights on the western half of the country. Based on the colors, the ATL community has memberships primarily from some airports on both the east and west coasts, but less directly in central USA. Therefore, it seems that the ATL community serves as a “hub” community connecting airports in the west coast to airports in the east coast – this intuition is justified by noting that ATL has the largest number of destinations out of any airport in the USA.

The January 2021 community seems to exhibit a combination of a form of seasonality together with COVID-19, though it is perhaps not as pronounced as the March 2020 seasonality effect, nor is it as pronounced as the August 2021 COVID-19 effect. To emphasize these effects, we plot this mode by combining it with March 2020 (to emphasize seasonality) and August 2021 (to emphasize the COVID-19 effect) in
Fig.

6
. When combined with August 2021, the COVID-19 effect becomes more pronounced during and after 2020. When combined with March 2020, the seasonality effect becomes even more pronounced before March 2020, with larger swings within each year. Both combinations further corroborate our finding that the January 2021 community exhibits both of these effects.

Figure 6
:
Joint plot emphasizing COVID-19 (left) and seasonality (right) effects of the January 2021 community.

C.4
More Discussion on the Global Trade Data Analysis

For the pure nodes associated to the USA and Canada, we see that the membership is relatively dispersed outside of Europe, which provides evidence that European trade communities are “closer-knit” than other communities. Since the USA and Canada likely have similar trading patterns, in
Fig.

7
we combine these two values, and we see that the memberships are fairly global besides Europe, though the intensity in any one area is not as strong as the intensities for the other pure nodes.

Figure 7
:
Combined memberships for the pure nodes associated to the USA and Canada.

Next we consider the pure nodes corresponding to the different goods. The pure nodes were found to be maize (corn), crude materials, distilled alcoholic beverages, food prep nes (not elsewhere specfied), and whole cow milk cheese. It was found in
Jing et al. 2021
that communities roughly correspond to either prepared or unprepared food; we also found food prep nes as one of the pure nodes, which gives further evidence to this finding. This community is also the “largest” community – the mean membership in this mode is .4147. To better understand the separation between processed and unprocessed food, we combine the “processed” communities food prep nes, distilled alcoholic beverages, and whole cow milk cheese into one community and group the other two communities together. Below is a summary of the communities with greater than
.7
membership intensity in either group, as well as those with smaller than
.7
intensity in both communities.

•

Processed
>.7
: Tobacco products nes, Butter (cowmilk), Tomatoes, Milk (skimmed, dried), Tobacco (unmanufactured ), Spices (nes), Fruit (prepared nes), Cigarettes, Potatoes, non alcoholic Beverages, Vegetables (frozen), Oil (essential nes), Oil (vegetable origin nes), Nuts (prepared (exc. groundnuts)), Sugar Raw Centrifugal, Vegetables (fresh nes), Waters (ice, etc.), Flour, wheat, Nuts nes, Tomato paste, Macaroni, Sugar refined, Food prep nes, Cheese (whole cow milk), Chocolate products nes, Beer of barley, Beverages (distilled alcoholic), Bread, Cereals (breakfast), Coffee extracts, Coffee (roasted), Fruit (dried nes), Apples, Flour (maize), Pastry, Sugar confectionery, Wine, Sugar nes.

•

Unprocessed
>.7
: Crude materials, Maize, palm oil, Sesame seed, Wheat

•

Neither
: milled Rice, dehydrated Vegetables, Pepper (piper spp.), chicken, Infant food, Fruit (fresh nes), Tea, Beans (dry), Coffee (green), dry Chillies and peppers, orange juice (single strength), soybean oil, fruit Juice nes, Milk (whole dried), Vegetables (preserved nes), Honey (natural).

By examining these “communities,” it seems that the processed foods are more similar than the unprocessed foods, since many more foods have higher memberships in communities associated to processed foods. Moreover, the “neither” category also contains some “mildly processed foods” (e.g., dried milk), which shows how the mixture model here is more representative of the data. We leave further investigations to future work.

Appendix D
Proof of Theorem
2

This section contains the full proof of Theorem
2
. Without loss of generality, throughout this section we assume that
\sigma=1
. Throughout we denote
\mathbf{T}_{k}=\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{T})
and
\mathbf{Z}_{k}
similarly.
We also let
p=p_{\max}
for convenience throughout the proofs.

Before proving our main results, we state the following results for the initialization. The proof is contained in
Section

D.5
.
It is worth noting that our
\ell_{2,\infty}
slightly sharpens the results of
Cai et al. 2021
by a factor of
\kappa^{2}
for the diagonal-deleted estimator; however, we do not consider missingness as they do. In what follows, we define the leave-one-out initialization
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,k-m)}
as the eigenvectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\Gamma\Big(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m})^{\top}\big),

where
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m}
denotes the matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{k}
with its
m
’th row set to zero (the double appearance of the index
k
will be useful for defining the other two leave-one-out sequences in the following subsection).

Theorem 4

(Initialization
\ell_{2,\infty}
error)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

2
. Then with probability at least
1-O(p^{-20})
, it holds for each
k
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{S}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{k}p_{-k}}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}+\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}};

\displaystyle\max_{m}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S})^{\top}-\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,k-m)}(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,k-m)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{k}p_{-k}}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}.

In
Section

D.1
we describe in detail the leave-one-out sequences for the iterates of tensor SVD. In
Section

D.2
we obtain the deterministic bounds needed en route to
Theorem

2
, and in
Section

D.3
we use these bounds to obtain high-probability guarantees on good events.
Section

D.4
contains the final proof of
Theorem

4
. Throughout we rely on several self-contained probabilistic lemmas, whose statements and proofs can be found in
Appendix

F
.

D.1
The Leave-One-Out Sequence

In this section we formally define the leave-one-out sequence. First, we already have defined
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}
and
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,k-m)}
in the previous section, but we will need a few additional pieces of notation. We define
\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{k}^{(t)}
as the output of tensor power iteration after
t
iterations, with
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(0)}=\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}
. It will also be useful to define

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)}}&k=1;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}&k=2;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}&k=3.\end{cases}

The matrix
\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}
is simply the projection matrix corresponding to the previous two iterates.

We have already defined the matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{j}^{j-m}
as the
j
’th matricization of
\mathcal{Z}
with its
m
’th row set to zero. We now define
\mathcal{Z}^{j-m}
as the corresponding tensor
\mathcal{Z}
, where the entries corresponding to the
m
’th row of
\mathbf{Z}_{j}
are set to zero. Finally, define
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\coloneqq\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{Z}^{j-m})
. In other words
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}
is the
k
’th matricization of the tensor
\mathcal{Z}
with the entries corresponding to the
m
’th row of
\mathbf{Z}_{j}
set to zero.

We now define
\widetilde{{\mathbf{U}}}_{k}^{(S,j-m)}
as the leading
r_{k}
eigenvectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}\big).

We now show that the other leave-one-out sequence initializations are sufficiently close to the true initialization.

Lemma 2

(Proximity of the initialization leave-one-out sequences)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

2
. Then the initializations of the leave-one-out sequences satisfy for each
k
the bound

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq 3}\max_{1\leq m\leq p_{j}}\|\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,j-m)}(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,j-m)})^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-19})
.

Lemma

2
is proven in
Section

D.5
after the proof of
Theorem

4
. To define subsequent iterates, we set
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
as the outputs of tensor power iteration using these initializations, though with one modification. We now define
\widetilde{{\mathbf{U}}}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
as the left singular vectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{k}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m},

which is still independent from
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{j}
. Here, we set
\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}
inductively as the projection matrix

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1,j-m)}}&k=1;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)}}&k=2;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)}}&k=3.\end{cases}

Note that for each
k
there are
3
different leave-one-out sequences, one corresponding to each mode, by leaving out the
m
’th row of that mode (note that for convenience we use the index
m
for each leave-one-out sequence, but we slightly abuse notation as
m
as defined above must satisfy
1\leq m\leq p_{j}
).

We now introduce some notation used for the remainder of our proofs. Define

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t)}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2};

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2};

\displaystyle\tau_{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})\leq 2r_{k+1}\\
\|\mathbf{U}_{2}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})\leq 2r_{k+2}\end{subarray}}\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}}\bigg)\|;

\displaystyle\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3\end{cases}

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3\end{cases}.

First we will state results deterministically with dependence on
\tau_{k}
,
\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
and
\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
. Note that we already have the bound
\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}\leq\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
since
\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\|=1
, but it will turn out to be slightly more useful to have the dependence on
\mathbf{V}_{k}
.

D.2
Deterministic Bounds

In this section we collect and prove deterministic bounds that we will then combine with probabilistic induction in
Section

D.3
.

Lemma 3

(Closeness of the orthogonal matrix)
.

Let
\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}=\mathrm{sgn}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})
be the matrix sign of
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
and
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}
. Then

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|^{2}.

Proof of Lemma
3
.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|^{2}.

For details on the final inequality, see Lemma 4.6.3 of
Chen et al. 2021
.
∎

Lemma 4

(Deterministic Bound for the Linear Term)
.

Suppose
\mathbf{T}_{k}={\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}
, and suppose that
\lambda/2\leq\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})
Then the linear term
\mathbf{L}_{k}^{(t)}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{k}\eta_{k}^{(t)}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|\bigg)+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\tau_{k}\eta_{k}^{(t,k-m)}\bigg)+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t,k-m},

Proof of Lemma
4
.

Without loss of generality we prove the result for
k=1
; the cases for
k=2
and
k=3
are similar by changing the index for
t
using the definition of
\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}
.

Recall we let
\mathbf{T}_{k}={\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}
. Then the
m
’th row of the linear term
\mathbf{L}_{1}^{(t)}
can be written as

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}.

Taking norms, we see that as long as
2\lambda^{-1}\geq(\widehat{\lambda}_{r_{1}}^{(t)})^{-1}
as in the assumptions of this lemma, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|.

(6)

Thus, it suffices to analyze the two terms

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|;

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|;

for fixed
m
. For the term
T_{1}
, we introduce the leave-one-out sequence to observe that

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg)\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)})\|+2\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|.

(7)

As for
T_{2}
, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

(8)

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg)\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|,

(9)

where the final line used the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathbf{V}_{1}=\mathbf{V}_{1}
by definition.

We now plug in the bound for
T_{1}
in (
7
) and
T_{2}
in (
9
) to the initial bound in (
6
) to obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)})\|+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t)}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t,1-m)}\bigg)+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t,1-m}

as desired.
∎

Lemma 5

(Deterministic Bound for the Quadratic Term)
.

Suppose
\lambda/2\leq\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}
). Then the quadratic term
\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{k}\eta_{k}^{(t)}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\bigg]\bigg\|\bigg)+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{k}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t,k-m)}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{k}^{t,k-m}\bigg(\tau_{k}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|+\tau_{k}\eta^{(t-1)}_{k}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\bigg\|\bigg).

Proof of Lemma
5
.

Similar to Lemma
4
we prove for
k=1
; the case for
k=2
or
k=3
follows by modifying the index of
t
according to the definition of
\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}.

Observe that
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}
is a projection matrix and hence equals its square. Therefore, we simply decompose by noting that under the condition that
\widehat{\lambda}_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})\geq\lambda/2

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1}}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1}}\bigg]\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{1-m,t-1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1}}\bigg]\bigg\|\bigg\|(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{1-m,t-1}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\tau_{1}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,1-m)}\bigg)+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}\bigg\|(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|.

Finally, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|

\displaystyle(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|\tau_{1}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|\tau_{1}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|+\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|+\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|+\tau_{1}\eta^{(t)}_{1}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg\|,

and, similarly,

\displaystyle\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t)}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\|.

Plugging in these bounds to our initial bound completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 6

(Eigengaps)
.

Suppose that
\tau_{k}\leq\lambda/4
and that

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

Then the following bounds hold:

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3\lambda}{4};

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda}{4}.

Proof.

Note that since
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}
is
\mathbf{Z}_{k}
with columns (or rows if
k=j
) removed, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\succcurlyeq(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}

and hence that

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\succcurlyeq\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}.

Taking norms, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{k}^{2},

where we took the supremum in the final inequality. Therefore,
\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\|\leq\tau_{k}
. Therefore, by Weyl’s inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\lambda_{r_{k}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)-\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda}{4}.

(10)

Next, when
\eta_{k}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{4}
, this implies that

\displaystyle\max\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1})\|,\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2})\|\bigg)\leq\frac{1}{4},

and hence that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2})^{\top}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda\lambda_{\min}\bigg(({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2})^{\top}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})

\displaystyle\geq\lambda(1-\frac{1}{16})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{15}{16}\lambda.

(11)

Combining (
11
) and (
10
) gives the first claim.

For the second claim, we simply note that by Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\bigg|\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)-\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{k}\leq\frac{\lambda}{4}.

Since
\mathbf{T}_{k}
is rank
r_{k}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)=0,

which proves the second assertion. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7

(Deterministic Bound for Leave-One-Out Sequence)
.

Suppose that
\tau_{k}\leq\frac{\lambda}{4}
and that
\eta_{k}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{4}
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\tau_{k}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\xi_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t,j-m}+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{k}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{k}\xi_{k}^{t,j-m}+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{k}^{t,j-m})^{2},

Proof.

We prove the result for
k=1
; the result for
k=2
and
k=3
are similar by modifying the index on
t
.

Recall that
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}
are the singular vectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}

and
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m}
are the singular vectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,j-m}

Consequently, the projection
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m}(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m})^{\top}
is also the projection onto the dominant left singular space of the matrix

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},

Therefore, both projections are projections onto the dominant eigenspaces of the matrices defined via

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{A}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\bigg[\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\bigg];

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widetilde{A}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\bigg[\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}\bigg].

Therefore, the perturbation
\mathbf{\widehat{A}-\widetilde{A}}
is equal to the sum of three terms, defined via

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{3}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}

where we have used the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}
is a projection matrix and hence equal to its square. We now bound each term successively.

The term
\|\mathbf{P}_{1}\|
:
Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{1}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\right)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\right)\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg)\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}.

Taking norms yields

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{1}\tau_{1}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\lambda_{1}\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\|\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+2\lambda_{1}\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\|\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\lambda_{1}\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{1}\tau_{1}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+2\lambda_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\lambda_{1}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t,j-m}.

For
\mathbf{P}_{2}
we proceed similarly. It holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg(\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t)}_{k+1}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg)\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m})^{\top}.

Taking norms yields the same upper bound as for
\|\mathbf{P}_{1}\|
.

For the the term
\mathbf{P}_{3}
, we note that since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}
is a projection matrix and hence equal to its cube, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{3}\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\right]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\left[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\right]\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\tau_{1}\bigg\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg\|\bigg\|\left[\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\right]\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\tau_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}+\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg\|\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}

\displaystyle\leq 4\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+2\tau_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}+\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\bigg)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{t-1,j-m}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{t-1,j-m}}\bigg\|\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}

\displaystyle\leq 4\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+2\tau_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}+\big(\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}\big)^{2}.

We note that by Lemma
6
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{1}}\big(\mathbf{\widetilde{A}}\big)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}\big)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r_{1}}^{2}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\frac{3}{4}\lambda\bigg)^{2}-\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{4}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4}.

Consequently, by the Davis-Kahan Theorem, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{P}_{1}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{2}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{3}\|\bigg),

which holds under the eigengap condition by Lemma
6
and the assumption
\tau_{k}\leq\frac{\lambda}{4}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{P}_{1}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{2}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{3}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(2\lambda_{1}\tau_{1}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+2\lambda_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\lambda_{1}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(4\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+2\tau_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}+\big(\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\tau_{1}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t,j-m}+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}\xi_{1}^{t,j-m}+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{1}^{t,j-m})^{2}

as desired.
∎

D.3
Probabilistic Bounds on Good Events

This section contains high-probability bounds for the terms considered in the previous subsection. Let
r=\max r_{k}
,
p=\max p_{k}
. In what follows, we denote

\displaystyle\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)},

where
C_{0}
is taken to be some fixed constant.

We will also recall the notation from the previous section:

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)}}&k=1;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}&k=2;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}&k=3.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1,j-m)}}&k=1;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)}}&k=2;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)}}&k=3.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t-1)}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t-1)})^{-2};

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{k}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t-1)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\tau_{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})\leq 2r_{k+1}\\
\|\mathbf{U}_{2}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})\leq 2r_{k+2}\end{subarray}}\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}}\bigg)\|;

\displaystyle\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3\end{cases}

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3\end{cases}.

We will also need to define several probabilistic events.
The first event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
collects several probabilistic bounds that hold independently of
t
, provided
t_{\max}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cp}
for some constant
c
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\max_{k}\tau_{k}\leq C\sqrt{pr}\bigg\}\bigcap\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\text{ for all $t\leq t_{\max}$ and $1\leq k\leq 3$ }\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\left(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\bigg\|\leq C\left(r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\sqrt{p_{k}}\bigg\}.

(12)

Lemma

19
demonstrates that the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\mathrm{Good}}}
holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
. We now define several events we use in our induction argument. Set

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)})\|\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigcap_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\Bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{2,\infty}\cap\bigcap_{j=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{j}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t,j}\Bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},1}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},1}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,3}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},2}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},2}.

The event
\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{2,\infty}
concerns the desired bound, the event
\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{j-m}
controls the leave one out sequences, and the other events
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}
are simply the intersection of these events, mainly introduced for convenience.

Finally, the following event concerns the incoherence of our leave-one-out sequences:

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{-k}}}.\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\bigcap\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}.\Bigg\},

where
c
is some deterministic constant.
While the precise definition of the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t,k}
is complicated, it is useful to keep in mind that the event will be used as an event independent of the nonzero elements in the matrices
\mathbf{Z}_{k}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}
, and it simply controls the incoherence of the leave-one-out sequences.

The following lemma shows that the leave-one-out sequences are incoherent whenever there are bounds on the previous iterates in
\ell_{2,\infty}
norm.

Lemma 8
.

For any fixed
t_{0}
,
j
,
k
, and
m
with
1\leq t_{0}\leq t_{\max}
,
1\leq j\leq 3
,
1\leq k\leq 3
, and
1\leq m\leq p_{j}
, it holds that the set

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}\cap\left(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},k}\right)^{c}

is empty.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we prove the result for
k=1
; the cases
k=2
and
k=3
are similar (in fact, the result can be made slightly sharper, but this is not needed for our purposes).

Note that when
t_{0}\geq 1
, it holds that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}
.

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}.

In addition, on this event it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}};

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}.

(13)

Next, observe that on the events listed,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}

\displaystyle\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\eqqcolon(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)+(VI),

where

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle(II):

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle(IV)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle(V)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle(VI)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}.

We now bound each term in turn, where we will use (
13
) repeatedly. We have that

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg\|\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

(14)

Similarly,

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg\|\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}.

Next,

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}}.

For the next two terms, we note that
for any orthogonal matrix
\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{O}(r_{k})
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{t_{0}-1\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{t_{0}-1\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|.

By taking the infimum over
\mathbb{O}(r_{2})
, we note that by Proposition 1 of
Cai and Zhang 2018

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{O}(r_{2})}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg),

where the final inequality is on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
since
t_{0}\leq t_{\max}
. We also note that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}
and the right-invariance of
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}
to orthogonal matrices,

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{O}(r_{2})}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{O}(r_{2})}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\mathbf{W}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{((2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle(IV)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle\leq 6\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle(V)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

Finally,

\displaystyle(VI)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}.

Plugging all of these bounds in we obtain (with
c=6
)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}.

This shows that the first part of the event in
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
must hold. For the second part of the event, we note that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg)\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg)\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1,j-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg)\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+4\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+4\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+4\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}.

where we used the fact that
\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{3}}}
on the events in question, and similarly for
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}
