. This shows the second part of the event must hold, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9

(Proximity of the Leave-one-out Sequence on a good event)
.

Let
1\leq j\leq 3
and
1\leq m\leq p_{j}
be fixed. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m})\|\geq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq p^{-29}.

Proof.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
it holds that
\tau_{k}\leq C\sqrt{pr}\ll\lambda
by assumption, since
r\leq Cp_{\min}^{1/2}
and
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa p/p_{\min}^{1/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
. Therefore, the eigengap assumption in Lemma
7
is met, so on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\tau_{k}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t_{0},j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t_{0},j-m)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{k}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{k}^{(t_{0},j-m)}\bigg)+\frac{8}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{k}\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m})^{2},

where we recall the notation

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{t-1,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\right)\right\|+\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{t-1,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)}\right)\right\|&k=1;\\
\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{t-1,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)}\right)\right\|+\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{t,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}\right)\right\|&k=2\\
\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{t,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)}\right)\right\|+\left\|\sin\Theta\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{t,j-m},\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}\right)\right\|&k=3;\end{cases};

\displaystyle\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\|\left(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\right)\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\right\|;

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\|\left(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\right)\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\right\|.

Similar to Lemma
8
we now complete the proof for
k=1
without loss of generality (if
k=2
or
3
, the proof is similar since slightly stronger bounds hold, but this again is not needed for our analysis). On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}
, we have the additional bounds

\displaystyle\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{pr};

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t_{0},j-m)}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg).

Plugging this in to the deterministic bound for
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m})\|
above yields

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16C_{1}\kappa}{\lambda}\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}+\frac{16C_{1}^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m})^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\frac{16C_{1}\kappa}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{2}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{3}\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg)\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}\sqrt{\frac{p_{2}}{p_{1}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}}{\lambda}+\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}\sqrt{\frac{p_{3}}{p_{1}}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg(\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}}{\lambda}+\frac{16C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{32C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}\sqrt{\frac{p}{p_{\min}}}}{\lambda}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}},\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg(\frac{32C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}},\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{32C_{1}C_{2}\kappa p/p_{\min}^{1/4}}{\lambda}\bigg\}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg(\frac{32C_{1}C_{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg),

which holds under the assumption
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}/r_{j}\leq C
, and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
. By a similar argument,

\displaystyle\frac{16C_{1}^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t,j-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}

\displaystyle\quad+\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m})^{2}.

The bound above depends only on
\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}
and
\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}
. Define

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg\{\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}(\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m})^{2};

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}.

Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\|

\displaystyle\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m})\|\geq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(I)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(II)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(III)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}.

We now will derive probabilistic bounds for each of the terms above on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}
. We will consider each term separately, though the strategy for each will remain the same: since there is nontrivial dependence between the events above and the random variable
\xi_{1}^{j-m}
, we use the auxiliary event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
, which is independent of the nonzero entries in the random matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}
. We then use Lemma
8
to show that the intersection of this event with other events is empty.

The term

(I)
:
We note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg\{(I)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(I)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\big(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\big)^{c}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(I)\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\},

where we have used Lemma
8
to show that the intesection of the complement
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
with the other events is zero.

Now we simply observe that
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},k}
does not depend on any of the random variables in the matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}
, so we are free to condition on this event. Recall that

\displaystyle\xi_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg\|.

By Lemma
17
, it holds that

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{p_{-j}\log(p)}\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
. On the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},k}
we have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg\}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\sqrt{p_{-j}\log(p)}\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+2\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+C^{\prime}\sqrt{p_{-j}\log(p)}\bigg(\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime\prime}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+C^{\prime\prime}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\Bigg\},

where we have absorbed the constants in each term. Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
it holds that

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime\prime}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\bigg\{\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+C^{\prime\prime}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\bigg\{\frac{16\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg\}.

We now show the first term is less than
\frac{1}{8}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}
and the second term is less than
\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}
. The first term will be less than this provided that

\displaystyle\frac{8}{C_{0}C^{\prime\prime}}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\bigg\{16\bigg(\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg)+\frac{8C_{1}\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda\kappa}\bigg\}

is less than one. This follows from basic algebra and the assumptions
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
, that
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
, and that
r_{k}\asymp r
. A similar argument shows that the second term is smaller than
\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}
. Therefore, on the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
it holds that

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}.

The term (II):
By a similar argument, we note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\left\{(II)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\right\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(II)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1})^{c}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(II)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\},

where again we used Lemma
8
. Conditioning on the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
, by the same argument as in Term
(I)
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
one has

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{j-m}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{C}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\Bigg\},

where we have once again absorbed the constant. Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
,

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\big(\xi_{1}^{j-m}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\widetilde{C}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\widetilde{C}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\sqrt{p_{-1}}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{4\widetilde{C}\sqrt{p_{-1}}}{C_{0}\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{4\widetilde{C}\sqrt{p_{-1}}}{C_{0}\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}},

where the final inequality holds when the additional terms are smaller than one, which holds via basic algebra as long as
C_{0}\geq 4C\widetilde{C}
,
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
.

The term (III):
Proceeding similarly again,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\left\{(III)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\right\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(III)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathcal{E}_{\text{Good }}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\text{main }}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1})^{c}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{(III)\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\right)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\},

where again we used Lemma
8
. Conditioning on the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
, by Lemma
17
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
one has

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{j-m}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{p_{-j}\log(p)}\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}.

On the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t_{0},1}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}.

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
,

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8C\kappa}{\lambda}\sqrt{p_{-j}\log(p)}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{64C^{\prime}}{C_{0}}\sqrt{p_{-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Bigg\{\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{j}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{3}r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{2}r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{1}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}},

where the final inequality holds by basic algebra as long as
C_{0}\geq 64CC^{\prime}
for some other constant
C^{\prime}
, as well as the assumptions
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
.

Consequently, we have shown that the desired bounds on the terms
(I)
,
(II)
, and
(III)
hold with probability at most
O(p^{-30})\leq p^{-29}
as desired.
∎

Lemma 10

(Bounding the linear term on a good event)
.

Let
t_{0}
and
k
be fixed, and let
m
be such that
1\leq m\leq p_{k}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{k}^{t_{0}}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}\Bigg\}\leq p^{-29}.

Proof of Lemma
10
.

The proof of this is similar to the proof of Lemma
9
, only using the deterministic bound in Lemma
4
instead of the deterministic bound in Lemma
7
. Once again without loss of generality we prove the result for
k=1
; the bounds for
k=2
and
k=3
are similar.

First, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, it holds that
\lambda/2\leq\lambda_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t_{0}-1)})
for
t_{0}\geq 1
. By Lemma
4
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{k}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t)}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\bigg)+\frac{8\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\tau_{k}\eta_{k}^{(t,k-m)}\bigg)+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t_{0},k-m}.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, we have the following bounds:

\displaystyle\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{pr};

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\big(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\big);

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{2}{2^{t_{0}-1}};

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t,1-m)}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{1}}}+\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{1}}}.

Plugging in these bounds yields

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{k}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8C\kappa}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{2}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8C\sqrt{pr}\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{1}}}+\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{1}}}\bigg)+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t_{0},k-m}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{8C\kappa}{\lambda}\Bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\Bigg]+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{8C\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\frac{2}{2^{t_{0}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{8C\sqrt{pr}\kappa}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{8C\sqrt{pr}\kappa}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{t_{0},k-m}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{8C}{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\frac{32C\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\frac{32C\sqrt{pr}{\kappa}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{r}{r_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)+\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m},

where the final inequality holds as long as

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{8C}{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)\Bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\Bigg]\leq\frac{1}{8}

and

\displaystyle\frac{32C\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}+\frac{32C\sqrt{pr}{\kappa}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{r}{r_{1}}}\leq\frac{1}{8}.

These two inequalities hold as long as
C_{0}
is larger than some fixed constant and the assumptions
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
.
Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}\bigcap(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1})^{c}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

where we have used Lemma
8
to conclude that the event in the penultimate line is empty. Therefore, it suffices to bound
\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}
on the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}
. Since this event is independent from the random variables belonging to
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}
, by Lemma
16
, it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}.

On the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}.

Therefore with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{4\kappa}{\lambda}\widetilde{\xi}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4C\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda}\sqrt{p_{-1}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{4C\sqrt{p_{-1}}}{C_{0}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{r_{1}}p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{r_{1}p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{r_{1}p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{r_{1}p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{r_{1}p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{1}{p_{-1}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}},

where the final inequality holds by similar algebraic manipulations as in the previous part of this proof provided that
C_{0}
is larger than some fixed constant together with the assumptions
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p_{\min}^{1/2}
.
This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 11

(Bounding the quadratic term on a good event)
.

The quadratic term satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\Bigg\}\leq p^{-29}.

Proof.

Again without loss of generality we prove the result for
k=1
; the bounds for
k=2
and
3
are similar. First, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
it holds that
\lambda/2\leq\lambda_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}^{(t_{0}-1)})
, and hence by Lemma
5
it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t)}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\|\bigg)+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}^{(t,1-m)}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}\bigg(\tau_{1}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|+\tau_{1}\eta^{(t-1)}_{1}+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg\|\bigg).

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, one has the following bounds:

\displaystyle\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{pr};

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t_{0},1-m)}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{2}{2^{t_{0}-1}};

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}};

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|

\displaystyle\leq C(r+\sqrt{\log(p)}).

Plugging these in yields

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4C}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{2}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16C^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg[\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{4C}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{3}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{4C}{C_{0}}\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{16C^{2}pr}{C_{0}\lambda\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)\bigg[\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg[\frac{32C\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{32C^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{32C^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\frac{4C}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{3}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\frac{4C}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{3}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg],

where the final inequality holds as long as

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{4C}{C_{0}}\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)\bigg[\sqrt{pr}

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{16C^{2}pr}{C_{0}\lambda\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}\bigg)\bigg[\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}\bigg]\leq\frac{1}{8}

and

\displaystyle\frac{32C\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{2}}{r_{1}}}\frac{32C^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}+\sqrt{\frac{r_{3}}{r_{1}}}\frac{32C^{2}pr}{\lambda^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{8}.

Both of these conditions hold when
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
r\leq Cp_{\min}^{1/2}
provided the constant
C_{0}
is larger than some fixed constant. Finally, we note that

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}

\displaystyle\frac{4C}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{3}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{4C}{\lambda}\bigg)\bigg[\sqrt{pr}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{3}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{C}\big(\sqrt{pr}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\big)}{\lambda}\bigg).

Define

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{C}\big(\sqrt{pr}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\big)}{\lambda}\bigg).

Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{B_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{B_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigcap(\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1})^{c}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{B_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg\}\bigcap\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}\Bigg\},

where we have used Lemma
8
to conclude that the event in the penultimate line is empty. Since the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}
is independent from the random variables belonging to
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}
, by Lemma
16
, it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
that

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}^{t_{0},1-m}_{1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}^{t_{0},1-m}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}.

On the event
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{1-m}^{t_{0},1}
, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}^{t_{0},1-m}_{1}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq c\Bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\Bigg\}.

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
, it holds that

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{C}\big(\sqrt{pr}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\big)}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{pr}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{pr}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\bigg\{\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\sqrt{p_{-1}}\frac{C_{0}}{C^{\prime}}\frac{\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}r_{1}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{C^{\prime\prime}\sqrt{p_{1}}\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)\bigg[\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}\sqrt{r_{1}}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}r_{1}}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}r_{1}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}},

where the final inequality holds as long as

\displaystyle\bigg(\sqrt{p_{-1}}\frac{C_{0}}{C^{\prime}}\frac{\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)\bigg(\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}\sqrt{r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+2\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(2)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}r_{1}}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(3)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{2}p_{3}r_{1}}}\bigg)\leq\frac{1}{8}

and

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{C^{\prime\prime}\sqrt{p_{1}}\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)\bigg[\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{p_{1}\sqrt{r_{1}}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{3}r_{1}}}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{2}r_{3}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}r_{1}}}\bigg]\leq\frac{1}{8},

both of which hold when
C_{0}
is larger than some fixed constant and
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\sqrt{\log(p)}p/p_{\min}^{1/4}
,
r_{k}\asymp r
and
r\leq Cp_{\min}^{1/2}
.
This completes the proof.
∎

D.4
Putting it all together: Proof of Theorem
2

Recall we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\max_{k}\tau_{k}\leq C\sqrt{pr}\bigg\}\bigcap\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\text{ for all $t\leq t_{\max}$ and $1\leq k\leq 3$ }\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\left(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\bigg\|\leq C\left(r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\sqrt{p_{k}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)})\|\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\Bigg[\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{-k}}}\Bigg].\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\bigcap\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}.\Bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigcap_{t=1}^{(t_{0}-1)}\Bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{2,\infty}\cap\bigcap_{j=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{j}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t,j}\Bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},1}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},1}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,3}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},2}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},2}.

Proof of Theorem
2
.

We will show that by induction that with probability at least
1-3(t_{0}+1)p^{-15}
that simultaneously for all
t\leq t_{0}
and each
k

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}};

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq m\leq p_{k}}\max_{1\leq j\leq 3}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},k-m})\|\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}.

Assuming that for the moment, suppose the algorithm is run for at most
C\log\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}}\bigg)
iterations. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\log(2^{t})

\displaystyle=\log\bigg(2^{C\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}})}\bigg)=C\log(2)\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}})\geq\log\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}}\bigg)

which in particular implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{t}}\leq\frac{C_{0}\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Moreover, from the assumption
\lambda\leq\exp(cp)
for some small constant
c
, it holds that

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle=C\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}})\leq C\log(\lambda)\leq cp,

and hence the event holds with probability at least

\displaystyle 1-(t-1)p^{-15}\geq 1-(cp-1)p^{-15}\geq 1-p^{-10}

provided
p
is sufficiently large.
Therefore, it remains to show that the result holds by induction.

Step 1: Base Case

By Theorem
4
it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-20})
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{S}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{1}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{k}p_{-k}}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}+\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{C\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}},

where the final inequality holds since
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa p\sqrt{\log(p)}p_{\min}^{1/4}
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\leq Cp_{\min}^{1/2}
and that
\kappa^{2}\leq cp_{\min}^{1/4}
as long as
c\times\sqrt{C}\leq\frac{1}{4}
. In addition, by Lemma
2
we have the initial bound for each
k
via

\displaystyle\max_{j}\max_{m}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{S},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{j-m})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{C\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-19})
.
Therefore, we have established the base case, which holds with probability
1-O(p^{-19})\geq 1-3p^{-15}
, as long as
C_{0}
in the definition of
\delta_{\mathrm{L}}
satisfies
C_{0}\geq C
, with
C
as above.

Step 2: Induction Step

Suppose that for all
t\leq t_{0}-1
it holds that with probability at least
1-3t_{0}p^{-15}
that

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}};

\displaystyle\max_{k}\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t,k-m})\|\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}.

Observe that the induction hypothesis is equivalent to stating that
\mathcal{E}_{1,\mathrm{main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}
holds with probability at least
1-3t_{0}p^{-15}
. We will now show that with probability at least
1-3t_{0}p^{-15}-p^{-15}
that
\mathcal{E}_{2,\mathrm{main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}
holds, which is equivalent to showing that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}};

\displaystyle\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{t_{0}}_{j},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}.

In other words, we will show that all of the bounds for the first mode hold.
Note that

\displaystyle\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{t_{0}}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{t_{0}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t_{0})})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{2}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{3}^{(t_{0}-1)}}\bigg]\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{1}^{t_{0}}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t_{0})})^{-2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}+\mathbf{L}_{1}^{t_{0}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}+{\mathbf{U}}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}+\mathbf{L}_{1}^{t_{0}}+{\mathbf{U}}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}).

Consequently,

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}+e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{1}^{t_{0}}+e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}).

We now proceed by bounding probabilistically. Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcup\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcup\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigg\}+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigg\}+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|_{2,\infty}\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\max_{m}\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+O(p^{-30})+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq p\max_{m}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\big)\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+p\max_{m}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},1-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+O(p^{-30})+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq p\max_{m}\Bigg[\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{L}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\big)\|\geq\frac{1}{4}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+p\max_{m}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\max_{j}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{t_{0}},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{t_{0},k-m}\|\geq\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{j}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{1,main}}^{(t_{0}-1)}\bigcap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+O(p^{-30})+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq 3p^{-28}+p^{-28}+O(p^{-30})+3t_{0}p^{-15}

\displaystyle\leq(3t_{0}+1)p^{-15},

for
p
sufficiently large, where the penultimate inequality holds by Lemmas
9
,
10
, and
11
, and the fact that on
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
,

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{t_{0}}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{t_{0}}\right)\|\leq\frac{1}{4}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\bigg)

by Lemma
3
since
t_{0}\leq\exp(cp)
by the assumption
\lambda\leq\exp(cp)
. Therefore, we have shown that the bound holds for
k=1
. For
k=2
, we proceed similarly, only now on the hypothesis that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}
holds with probability at least
1-3t_{0}p^{-15}-p^{-15}
.
The exact same argument goes through,accumulating an additional factor of
p^{-15}
. Finally, for
k=3
, we proceed again, only now assuming that
\mathcal{E}^{t_{0}-1,3}_{\mathrm{main}}
holds with probability at least
1-3t_{0}p^{-15}-2p^{-15}
. This accumulates a final factor of
p^{-15}
. Therefore, since this accumulates three factors of
p^{-15}
, it holds that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}
holds with probability at least
1-3(t_{0}+1)p^{-15}
as desired, which completes the proof.
∎

D.5
Initialization Bounds

This section contains the proof the initialization bounds.
Section

D.5.1
contains preliminary lemmas and their proofs,
Section

D.5.2
contains the proof of
Theorem

4
, and
Section

D.5.3
contains the proof of
Lemma

2
.

D.5.1
Preliminary Lemmas

The following result establishes concentration inequalities for the spectral norm of the noise matrices, needed in order to establish sufficient eigengap conditions.

Lemma 12
.

The following bounds hold simultaneously with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30}):

1.

\|{\rm diag}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})\|\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\frac{\log(p)}{p_{1}}}
;

2.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|\lesssim(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}

3.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|\lesssim\lambda_{1}\sqrt{p_{1}}
;

4.

\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\lesssim\sqrt{r}
.

Proof.

Part one follows since

\displaystyle\|{\rm diag}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle=\max_{i}\big|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}e_{i}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\|e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\lambda_{1}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\frac{\log(p)}{p_{1}}},

where the final inequality holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
by
Lemma

17
.

Part two follows by a slight modification of Lemma 1 of
Agterberg et al. 2022b
(with
M
in the statement therein taken to be 0), where the higher probability holds by adjusting the constant in the definition of
\delta
in the proof therein. We omit the detailed proof for brevity.

Part three follows since

\displaystyle\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|+\|{\rm diag}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\frac{\log(p)}{p_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\frac{\log(p)}{p_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}\lambda_{1},

where the penultimate inequality
\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}
holds by a standard
\varepsilon-
net argument, and the final inequality holds since
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim\sqrt{p_{\min}}
by assumption.

Part four follows via a standard
\varepsilon
-net argument.
∎

We also have the following result, needed in establishing the concentration of the leave-one-out sequences.

Lemma 13
.

The following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
:

1.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top})\|\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}
;

2.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top})\|\lesssim(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}

3.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top})\|\lesssim\mu_{0}\lambda_{1}\sqrt{r\log(p)}\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}}{p_{-1}}}
;

4.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top})\|\lesssim p
.

5.

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top})\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{S,1-m,1-l}\|\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}\log(p)\|\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{S,1-m,1-l}\|_{2,\infty}

Here
\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}
is the matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{1}
with its
m
’th row and
l
’th column removed, and
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{S,1-m,1-l}
is matrix of leading eigenvectors obtained by initializing with the noise matrix
\mathbf{Z}_{1}
replaced with
\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}
.

Proof of
Lemma

13
.

For part one, we observe that
\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}
is a zero matrix with only its
m
’th row nonzero. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|+\|{\rm diag}\big((\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle=\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|+\max_{i}|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}\big)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}e_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\lambda_{1}+\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\sqrt{p_{-1}\log(p)}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)},

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
, where the penultimate line follows from
Lemma

16
.

For part 2, we first observe that
\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}\big)
is a matrix with
i,j
entry equal to

\displaystyle\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}\big)_{ij}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\langle e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1},e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\rangle&i=m,j\neq m\\
\langle e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1},e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\rangle&j=m,i\neq m\\
0&\text{else}.\end{cases}

Therefore, we can decompose this matrix via

\displaystyle\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{row}}+\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{col}},

where
\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{row}}
is the matrix whose only nonzero row is its
m
’th row, in which case it the
m,j
entry is
\langle e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1},e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\rangle
for
j\neq m
, and
\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{col}}
is defined as the transpose of this matrix. We then observe that with high probability

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{row}}\|+\|\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{col}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{row}}\|

\displaystyle=2\|e_{m}^{\top}\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2},

where the final inequality follows from
Lemma

12
.

For part three, we first observe that
\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}
is a matrix with only its
l
’th column nonzero. Then we note

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\big(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\big)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p_{1}}\|\Gamma\big(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\big)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\big)\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p_{1}}\bigg[\|\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\big)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|{\rm diag}\bigg(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\big)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\bigg)\|_{2,\infty}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p_{1}}\bigg[\max_{i}\|\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big)_{il}\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{l\cdot}\|+\max_{i}|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il}(\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})_{li}|\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}\bigg[\sqrt{\log(p)}\|\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\|\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\|\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}\log(p)}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\lambda_{1}\sqrt{r\log(p)}\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}}{p_{-1}}},

where we used the fact that
\max_{i,l}|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il}|\lesssim\sqrt{\log(p)}
with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
.

We next note that
\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top}\big)
is a matrix with entries equal to
(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{jl}
for
i\neq j
and
i,j\neq m
. In particular, it is a the
p_{1}-1\times p_{1}-1
dimensional submatrix of the matrix whose entries are simply
(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{jl}
for
i\neq j
. This is a sample Gram matrix, so by Lemma 1 of
Agterberg et al. 2022b
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim p_{1}

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
(where as in the proof of
Lemma

12
the result holds by taking
M=0
,
d=1
, and modifying the constant on
\delta
in the proof of Lemma 1 of
Agterberg et al. 2022b
).

For the final term, we note that

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top})\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{S,1-m,1-l}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{a}\big\|\sum_{j\neq a}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{al}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{jl}\big(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}^{S,1-m,1-l}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{a}|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|_{al}\|\sum_{j\neq a}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{jl}\big(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}^{S,1-m,1-l}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\log(p)}\max_{a}\|\sum_{j\neq a}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{jl}\big(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}^{S,1-m,1-l}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p_{1}}\log(p)\|\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{S,1-m,1-l}\|_{2,\infty},

where the final inequality follows from
Lemma

16
applied to
\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}
.
∎

The following result verifies the eigengap conditions that we use repeatedly throughout the proof. We adopt similar notation to
Cai et al. 2021
.

Lemma 14
.

Define the matrices

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\big);

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{(m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m})^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m})^{\top})\big);

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{(m,l)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m-l})^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m-l}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m-l}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{-m-l})^{\top})\big).

Then on the events in
Lemma

12
and
Lemma

13
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}^{2}-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|\gtrsim\lambda^{2};

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-\lambda_{r+1}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G}^{-m}\|\gtrsim\lambda^{2};

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}^{-m}\big)-\lambda_{r+1}\big(\mathbf{G}^{-m}\big)-\|\mathbf{G}^{-m}-\mathbf{G}^{-m-l}\|\gtrsim\lambda^{2};

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)\gtrsim\lambda^{2}.

Proof of
Lemma

14
.

First, we note that on the event in
Lemma

12
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\rm diag}(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top})\|+2\|\Gamma(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|+\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p_{1}}+\lambda_{1}\sqrt{p_{1}}+(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda^{2};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G}^{-m}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\Gamma(\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top})\|+\|\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}+(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda^{2};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}^{-m}-\mathbf{G}^{-m-l}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l})^{\top}\big)\|+\left\|\Gamma\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}\right)^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m,1-l}\right)^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\lambda_{1}\sqrt{r\log(p)}\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}}{p_{-1}}}+p_{1}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda^{2}.

Therefore, by Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-\lambda_{r+1}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r}^{2}-3\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda^{2}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}^{-m}\big)-\lambda_{r+1}\big(\mathbf{G}^{-m}\big)-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G}^{-m}\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-\lambda_{r+1}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)-2\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G}^{-m}\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda^{2}.

Finally, we note that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\big(\mathbf{G}\big)\geq\lambda_{r}^{2}-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{2}-\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda^{2}.

This completes the proof.
∎

With these spectral norm concentration and eigengap conditions fixed, we now consider the
\ell_{2,\infty}
analysis. The first step is to show that several terms are negligible with respect to the main bound. For the remainder of the analysis, we implicitly use the eigenvalue bounds in
Lemma

14
, which hold under the events in
Lemma

12
and
Lemma

13
.

Lemma 15
.

The following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
:

1.

\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{S}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{\sqrt{r}\kappa}{\lambda}

2.

\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\Gamma(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{S}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}^{-2}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{1}}{p_{1}}}\frac{(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3})^{1/2}}{\lambda^{2}}

3.
